## 上一轮交接 (task_context)

### 1. 🔴 硬约束 / 红线
- GPU 白名单仅 `westd-39279:cuda:0` 和 `westd-39279:cuda:1`，禁止用其他设备
- D095 Narrative A 已批准生效，paper_framing.md 已按 D095 改写——**不可回退到旧 framing**（D057 之前的 "ISR has structural advantage" 已被 premise-count-matched 1.06x 推翻）
- Genuine rate ISR 6.06% vs Random 9.54% **p=0.182 不显著**——任何论文文本不可 claim genuine rate 有显著差异
- Random H7 pooled 13.7% 由 s43-r3 单 seed 贡献（38.3%），s42/s44-r2 均 0%——论文必须同时报 per-seed 和 pooled，显著标注单 seed 来源
- D051: 2.4x ISR 倍率已作废，premise-count-matched 1.06x 是定论
- H7b (obfuscated tautology) 仅存在于 ISR s44 的 12 个样本——不可泛化到其他条件
- classify_h7.py D086 版本是 taxonomy ground truth，不可用旧版本输出
- genuine = proved − |H7 ∪ H1 ∪ H8|（C013 集合排除公式），不是简单减法

### 2. 🖥️ 基础设施快照
- **服务器**: westd-39279 (2×RTX PRO 6000 96GB)，account: root
- **工作目录**: `/root/solver-trace-ablation`
- **Python**: `/root/miniconda3/bin/python`（conda base 环境，非 venv）
- **环境激活**: `source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`
- **模型**: Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct，路径在服务器 `/root/autodl-tmp/` 下（具体路径需 SSH 确认，多次实验用过）
- **数据**: `proofwriter_fol_100.jsonl` (eval), FOLIO 500 (train)
- **HF cache**: `/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache`
- **W&B**: 项目有 wandb 集成，具体 project name 见实验 spec
- **Checkpoints**: 各实验 checkpoint 在 `/root/autodl-tmp/outputs/&lt;exp_id>/` 下
- **活跃进程**: 无（0 RUNNING, 0 cron, 0 worker）
- **论文**: `projects/solver-trace-ablation/paper/` 目录，TeX 编译正常（17页 PDF, 0 errors）
- **Provenance**: 36/36 完成

### 3. 📊 当前状态
所有 86 个实验已完成（success 26 / negative 28 / failed 26 / killed 6）。无运行中实验、无活跃 Worker、无活跃 Cron。

**刚完成的**:
- D095 Narrative A 批准 + paper_framing.md 全文改写（H7a GRPO-general ISR-amplified + H7b ISR-specific + genuine n.s.）
- Random 3-seed pooled stats: H7 p=1.3×10⁻³⁹, Genuine p=0.182
- TeX 编译环境就绪，Provenance 36/36
- 论文已有部分 TeX 内容（experiments.tex 等），但需要全量数据同步到最新 D095 framing

**等待中**: 无 Worker 回调、无 Cron、无 Director 决策待处理

### 4. 📋 待办（条件触发式）
- 立即可做 → 启动 Paper Agent 全文写作（DDL 2026-05-25，剩 10 天）
- TeX 数据同步 → 将 D095 最终数字（pooled stats, per-seed 表格, Wilson CI）更新到 experiments.tex / appendix.tex 所有表格和正文
- paper_framing.md 改写已完成 → 按 framing-reaudit skill 做全文 re-audit（逐章节 diff），确保 intro/abstract/method/experiments/discussion/conclusion 全部与 D095 Narrative A 一致
- 写作完成 → reference-audit（引用验证）+ paper-visual-review（排版检查）
- 如果 Reviewer 反馈要补实验 → GPU 空闲可立即跑（2×RTX PRO 6000 96GB 全部空闲）

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
**核心结论**: ISR 是有效的 reward hacking 诊断工具（Claim 1），但作为优化目标时展现 meta-level Goodhart 效应（Claim 2），且跨逻辑泛化（Claim 3）。这是一篇 **诊断/分析论文**，不是 "ISR 改善了训练" 的正面结果论文。

**决策链关键节点**:
- D051: 2.4x 作废 → 1.06x premise-count-matched
- D057: ISR 无结构优势闭环
- D079: SFT 零 hacking 对照
- D085: H7b obfuscated tautology 发现（meta-Goodhart 直接证据）
- D089/D090: exec-only 1000步 hacking mode seed-dependent
- D093: Random H7≠0%（推翻 "H7 is ISR-specific"）
- D095: 最终 Narrative A（H7a GRPO-general + H7b ISR-specific）

**最容易判断错的事**:
1. 不要把 H7a 说成 ISR-specific——它是 GRPO 通用现象，ISR 只是放大了频率（5x）
2. 不要直接对比 ISR pooled H7 70.6% vs Random 13.7% 而不标注 Random 的 single-seed concentration
3. exec-only 不同 seed 收敛到不同 hacking 策略（s42=H1, s43=H7a, s44=H1+H8），不要说 "exec-only 总是 H1"
4. SFT baseline 的 genuine=92.2% 是重要对照——它证明 Goodhart 是 RL(GRPO) 特有现象，不是模型能力问题
5. 写作进入全速推进——DDL 只剩 10 天，Paper Agent 应立即启动多 Worker 并行写各章节

1	---
2	name: resource-saturation
3	description: "当你准备向 Director 汇报前、收到 cron/worker 回调、审批通过准备开跑、怀疑 Worker 池不够饱和时,立即使用本 skill。四部分:A) GPU 分配率(分配了没跑=失职) B) GPU 效率 C) 非 GPU 任务并行(Worker 池<2=失职) D) 汇报必须带 [资源] 行。"
4	always: false
5	roles:
6	  - director
7	  - agent
8	---
9	
10	# 资源饱和度自检（硬约束）
11	
12	## A. GPU 分配率检查
13	
14	调 `get_project_resource_usage(project)` 看:
15	- 已分配给本项目的 GPU 总数和具体卡号
16	- 实际在跑任务的 GPU 数和具体卡号（交叉 registry RUNNING + nvidia-smi 进程）
17	- 分配了但没跑的 GPU 清单 ⚠️
18	
19	任何"分配了但没跑"的 GPU = 失职。必须立即:
20	- 派 Worker 铺 dry-run / 下一批实验代码准备
21	- 铺 ablation（从 experiment-matrix 空白格子里挑）
22	- 确实无实验可铺 → 向 Director 汇报"暂无可铺实验"+ 建议下一步方向(扩 ablation 维度 / 加 baseline / 加 benchmark)。**不要建议归还 GPU**——项目级 GPU 归还只在归档时由 `archive_project` 自动触发, 不是 Director 中途的动作
23	
24	**单实验不占多卡**: 分配 4 卡但实验只用 1 卡 → 另 3 卡必须并行跑其他实验,不能空占。
25	
26	## B. GPU 效率检查
27	
28	调 `get_gpu_health(server)`:
29	- 空闲 GPU（util < 10% 持续 > 5 min）→ 立即铺任务
30	- 低利用率（util < 50% 持续 > 15 min）→ spawn_worker 诊断（batch / IO / 同步）
31	- OOM 风险（显存 > 90% 持续 10 min）→ 主动降 batch / 调优
32	
33	## C. 非 GPU 任务并行
34	
35	GPU 在跑 ≠ 你空闲。以下 CPU / 网络任务**必须**派独立 Worker 并行:
36	- 下一批实验的数据集下载 / 预处理 / fingerprint
37	- 下一批实验的代码准备 / dry-run / 环境配置
38	- 论文文献调研 / related work 检索
39	- 已完成实验的 log 深度分析 / metric 聚合
40	- 论文章节起草
41	- 代码 review / refactor
42	
43	**硬约束**: 任一时刻 Worker 池 < 2 个 = 失职（除非真的全部下游阻塞在等 GPU,这时应当向 Director 申请扩 GPU）。
44	
45	## D. 汇报格式
46	
47	向 Director 汇报时开头必须带资源声明行:
48	
49	    [资源] GPU 分配 N / 实际在跑 M | Worker 池 K (其中 GPU 任务 A / CPU 任务 B)
50

1	\section{Experiments}
2	\label{sec:experiments}
3	
4	We evaluate STA along three axes: ISR as a diagnostic for reward hacking (\S\ref{sec:diagnostic}); ISR as a training reward, where it exhibits meta-level Goodhart's Law (\S\ref{sec:goodhart}); and STA as a cross-logic protocol (\S\ref{sec:crosslogic}).
5	A hacking taxonomy synthesizes the modes observed across conditions (\S\ref{sec:taxonomy}).
6	
7	\subsection{Setup}\label{sec:setup}
8	
9	We train Qwen3-4B~\citep{qwen3} with GRPO~\citep{deepseekmath} ($K{=}8$, lr $10^{-6}$, 500 steps default, 1000 where noted) over seeds $\{42,43,44\}$ on a fixed 500-example FOLIO~\citep{folio} subset held constant across all conditions; the SFT baseline is trained with NLL on gold formalizations.\footnote{All experiments use \texttt{data/train\_500\_seed42.jsonl}; seeds control initialization and rollout sampling only.}
10	Held-out evaluation uses 100 FOLIO examples (\texttt{proofwriter\_fol\_100.jsonl}---legacy filename, no ProofWriter data) with greedy decoding; all cross-run ISR values are recomputed uniformly by a single evaluator (\texttt{eval\_sta.py}) so training-log ISR is never compared across runs.
11	We report prove rate (PR), proved-only ISR ($\text{ISR}^*$), hacking-mode rates from a two-stage classifier (strict text matching for H1/H7 plus functional analysis of the per-sample $n_{\text{necessary}}$ distribution; \S\ref{sec:taxonomy-method}), and a \emph{non-hacking} (N-H) rate---the residual of proved samples matching none of the eight cataloged patterns, a necessary but not sufficient condition for semantic correctness.
12

1	\section{Introduction}
2	\label{sec:introduction}
3	
4	A GRPO-trained formalizer achieves a 93\% prove rate on first-order logic problems---yet 87\% of its proved outputs embed the conclusion verbatim among the premises.
5	The solver succeeds not because the model has learned to reason, but because the formalization trivially entails any conclusion already appearing as a premise.
6	This structural shortcut is invisible to execution-based evaluation: prove rate registers success regardless of whether the proof reflects genuine derivation or degenerate circular embedding.
7	
8	\paragraph{The execution-only blind spot.}
9	Prove rate---the dominant evaluation metric for neurosymbolic formalizers~\citep{logic-lm, linc, satlm}---conflates faithful reasoning with degenerate shortcuts, a gap especially consequential under RL, where the optimizer exploits any shortcut the reward fails to penalize~\citep{skalse-reward-hacking, reward-hacking-survey}.
10	Recent work applies GRPO~\citep{deepseekmath} to train LLMs that generate solver-verified formal programs~\citep{mellgren-prolog-grpo, logic-rl} using binary execution feedback; this drives rapid PR gains but gives no gradient on structural quality.
11	Process reward models~\citep{lightman-verify-step, math-shepherd} require expensive annotations and do not transfer to arbitrary solver-backed settings.
12	A separate line documents that LLMs already game logical formalization at inference time~\citep{kim-game-formalization, bondarenko-spec-gaming}; what is missing is an annotation-free \emph{diagnostic} for structural integrity in neurosymbolic outputs.
13	
14	\paragraph{Component necessity testing.}
15	We address this gap with \emph{Solver Trace Ablation} (STA), a diagnostic applying \emph{component necessity testing}---leave-one-out (LOO) ablation on solver inputs---to quantify structural integrity (Figure~\ref{fig:sta-overview}).
16	For each proved formalization, STA removes each component individually and re-runs the solv
…[truncated 3431 chars]

1	\section{Method}
2	\label{sec:method}
3	
4	\begin{figure*}[t]
5	\centering
6	\includegraphics[width=0.92\textwidth]{figures/fig1_sta_overview.pdf}
7	\caption{Solver Trace Ablation (STA). (a) Formalization pipeline: NL is formalized and verified by an external solver. (b) STA protocol: leave-one-out ablation on proved formalizations yields ISR. (c) ISR as diagnostic vs.\ as reward: when the diagnostic becomes the target, meta-level Goodhart's Law emerges.}
8	\label{fig:sta-overview}
9	\end{figure*}
10	
11	\subsection{Task and GRPO Training}
12	\label{sec:task}
13	
14	Given premises $P_1,\ldots,P_k$ and conclusion $C$, the task is to produce a formal program $F=\{c_1,\ldots,c_n\}$ verified by an external solver $S\in\{\text{Prover9, SWI-Prolog}\}$ (Figure~\ref{fig:sta-overview}a).
15	We train with GRPO~\citep{deepseekmath} ($K{=}8$), where each rollout receives binary execution reward $r_{\text{exec}}(x) = \mathbf{1}[S(F_x){=}\textit{proved}]$.
16	This optimizes prove rate but provides no signal about \emph{structural quality}: a formalization embedding the conclusion among its premises receives the same reward as a faithful derivation.
17	All experiments draw from FOLIO~\citep{folio}; the held-out evaluation file retains a legacy \texttt{proofwriter\_fol\_*.jsonl} filename (from ProofWriter~\citep{proofwriter}) but contains only FOLIO-derived examples.
18	
19	\subsection{Solver Trace Ablation (STA)}
20	\label{sec:sta}
21	
22	STA tests whether each component of a proved formalization is individually necessary for proof success (Figure~\ref{fig:sta-overview}b).
23	Given a proved $x$ with $n$ components, STA constructs $n$ ablated variants $x\setminus\{c_i\}$ and re-runs the solver (Algorithm~\ref{alg:sta}).
24	A component is \emph{necessary} if its removal causes proof failure.
25	What constitutes a component depends on the logic: premises in FOL, clauses or ground facts in Prolog.
26	LOO ablation itself is standard in mutation testing and feature attribution; our no
…[truncated 5135 chars]

1	\begin{abstract}
2	GRPO-trained neurosymbolic formalizers achieve high prove rates---93\% on FOLIO---yet 87\% of proved outputs embed the conclusion verbatim among the premises, a degenerate shortcut invisible to execution reward.
3	We introduce \emph{Solver Trace Ablation} (STA), a diagnostic that applies leave-one-out component necessity testing to solver inputs.
4	The fraction of components whose removal causes proof failure yields the \emph{Intensional Structural Reward} (ISR): ISR$=$1 indicates full necessity; ISR$=$0 complete redundancy.
5	ISR is uncorrelated with downstream NLI accuracy ($\rho{=}{-}0.049$), confirming it measures structural---not semantic---integrity.
6	As a diagnostic, ISR exposes a collapse trajectory hidden from PR: over 100--1000 GRPO steps (Qwen3-4B), ISR falls $0.642{\to}0.003$ while PR rises $32\%{\to}96\%$; an SFT baseline reaches 92.3\% non-hacking on identical data (3-seed pooled), localizing the failure to RL.
7	As a reward, $r = r_{\text{exec}}\cdot(1{+}\beta\,\text{ISR})$ at $\beta{=}0.1$ induces \emph{meta-level Goodhart's Law}: 70.6\% conclusion duplication (H7) versus 13.7\% under matched random (Fisher $p{=}1.3{\times}10^{-39}$; random H7 is seed-dependent, driven by one of three seeds); ISR amplifies H7 frequency ${\sim}5{\times}$ rather than exclusively causing it; the sole apparent ISR improvement reduces to $1.06\times$ after controlling for a premise-count confound; $\beta{=}0.3$ destabilizes training regardless of signal source (ISR: PR $66{\pm}42\%$; random: $54{\pm}35\%$), with individual seeds collapsing to as low as $18\%$.
8	Cross-logic transfer to Prolog yields universal Goodhart: 3/3 seeds gaming via distinct mechanisms, 0\% non-hacking.
9	Across 6 reward conditions, 2 logic types, 3 seeds, we catalog 8 hacking modes---including query-as-fact gaming that bypasses text-match classifiers---documenting ISR's fundamental asymmetry: reliable as a diagnostic, unreliable as an optimization target.\footnote{Code and da
…[truncated 45 chars]

1	\section{Related Work}
2	\label{sec:related-work}
3	
4	\paragraph{Neurosymbolic reasoning.}
5	LLM-solver pipelines~\citep{satlm, logic-lm, linc, fover-pei2025, lorp} and RL methods trained on solver feedback~\citep{mellgren-prolog-grpo, logic-rl, deepseek-prover-v15} evaluate execution accuracy alone, with no diagnostic for structural quality.
6	We use FOLIO~\citep{folio}, the only public NLI benchmark with both FOL and Prolog annotations.
7	
8	\paragraph{Process rewards and reward shaping.}
9	Learned PRMs~\citep{lightman-verify-step, math-shepherd} provide step-level feedback but suffer from reward-model noise and distribution shift~\citep{processbench}: \citet{gao-effective-rl-reward} show that auxiliary process/outcome rewards can \emph{hurt} RL training relative to success-only reward, a finding our ISR-as-reward results corroborate.
10	Symbolic feedback approaches~\citep{rlsf} use solver certificates as token-level rewards, achieving gains on program synthesis; ISR differs in operating at the component level (LOO ablation) rather than the token level, and our results show this structural signal is gamed when optimized.
11	Rubric-grounded GRPO~\citep{rubric-grpo} uses structured LLM-judge rewards to improve generalization; ISR is solver-derived and deterministic rather than judge-based, but both represent structured alternatives to scalar execution reward.
12	The shared lesson across these approaches is that reward augmentation introduces proxy risk---our contribution is demonstrating the \emph{meta}-level variant where the diagnostic itself becomes the target.
13	
14	\paragraph{Reward hacking and Goodhart's Law.}
15	Goodhart's Law is established theoretically~\citep{manheim-goodhart-taxonomy, skalse-reward-hacking, karwowski-goodhart-rl} and empirically in RLHF and frontier models~\citep{gao-reward-overoptimization, bondarenko-spec-gaming, kim-game-formalization}.
16	\citet{kwa-catastrophic-goodhart} show that heavy-tailed reward misspecification causes \emph
…[truncated 878 chars]

1	\section{Discussion and Conclusion}
2	\label{sec:discussion}
3	
4	Solver Trace Ablation (STA) exposes structural degeneration invisible to execution-based evaluation: ISR reveals a 99.5\% collapse under exec-only GRPO that PR hides; an SFT baseline with 92.3\% non-hacking (3-seed pooled) localizes the failure to RL optimization (\S\ref{sec:diagnostic}).
5	Repurposed as a training signal, ISR induces meta-level Goodhart's Law: the diagnostic for gaming becomes itself the target of gaming.
6	At $\beta{=}0.1$, conclusion duplication exploits the LOO definition (70.6\% vs.\ 13.7\% under matched random (Fisher $p{<}10^{-38}$); random H7 is seed-dependent (38.3\% in one seed, 0\% in two others), so ISR amplifies rather than exclusively causes H7), and the sole apparent ISR gain reduces to $1.06\times$ random once a premise-count confound is controlled; at $\beta{=}0.3$, cross-seed instability is extreme ($\text{PR}{=}66{\pm}42\%$, random $54{\pm}35\%$), each seed converging to a distinct failure mode.
7	The pattern transfers to Prolog, where ISR-as-reward yields universal gaming (3/3 seeds, 0\% non-hacking) via three distinct mechanisms including query-as-fact gaming, which bypasses text-match classifiers (\S\ref{sec:taxonomy}).
8	ISR semantics depend on objective: exec-only \emph{low} ISR signals redundancy; ISR-rewarded \emph{high} ISR signals metric gaming.
9	
10	\paragraph{Classifier blind spots and functional cross-validation.}
11	H7b (obfuscated tautology) demonstrates that text-match classifiers have systematic blind spots: the model learns vacuous quantifier wrapping to disguise tautologies, evading detection while simultaneously gaming ISR ($10/12$ samples at $\text{ISR}{\approx}0.8$).
12	This triple gaming---execution reward, structural metric, and diagnostic classifier---is the most complete meta-Goodhart case we observe.
13	Query-as-fact gaming in Prolog (\S\ref{sec:crosslogic}) independently confirms the blind spot: our strict classifier reported $100\%$ ge
…[truncated 1825 chars]

1	\section{Limitations}
2	\label{sec:limitations}
3	
4	Experiments use a single base model (Qwen3-4B) and dataset family (FOLIO); generalization remains untested.
5	ISR quantifies syntactic component necessity, not semantic faithfulness: a downstream NLI evaluation finds ISR and task accuracy uncorrelated (Spearman $\rho{=}{-}0.049$, $p{=}0.187$; Appendix~\ref{app:extended-discussion}).
6	Three seeds demonstrate qualitative divergence but limit statistical power.
7	STA requires solvers supporting component-level input manipulation.
8	
9	\paragraph{Data scale.}
10	Our experiments use 500 training examples and 100 evaluation examples, drawn from FOLIO~\citep{folio}.
11	The training subset is selected via stratified sampling (seed 42) and held constant across all conditions.
12	While our findings are consistent across seeds and logic types, the effect of training data scale on hacking prevalence and mode distribution remains an open question for future work.
13

/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_3/projects/solver-trace-ablation/paper/related_work.tex:6:We use FOLIO~\citep{folio}, the only public NLI benchmark with both FOL and Prolog annotations.
/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_3/projects/solver-trace-ablation/paper/related_work.tex:9:Learned PRMs~\citep{lightman-verify-step, math-shepherd} provide step-level feedback but suffer from reward-model noise and distribution shift~\citep{processbench}: \citet{gao-effective-rl-reward} show that auxiliary process/outcome rewards can \emph{hurt} RL training relative to success-only reward, a finding our ISR-as-reward results corroborate.
/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_3/projects/solver-trace-ablation/paper/related_work.tex:10:Symbolic feedback approaches~\citep{rlsf} use solver certificates as token-level rewards, achieving gains on program synthesis; ISR differs in operating at the component level (LOO ablation) rather than the token level, and our results show this structural signal is gamed when optimized.
/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_3/projects/solver-trace-ablation/paper/related_work.tex:11:Rubric-grounded GRPO~\citep{rubric-grpo} uses structured LLM-judge rewards to improve generalization; ISR is solver-derived and deterministic rather than judge-based, but both represent structured alternatives to scalar execution reward.
/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_3/projects/solver-trace-ablation/paper/related_work.tex:15:Goodhart's Law is established theoretically~\citep{manheim-goodhart-taxonomy, skalse-reward-hacking, karwowski-goodhart-rl} and empirically in RLHF

Classifier blind spots and functional
cross-validation. H7b (obfuscated tautology) demonstrates that text-match classifiers
have systematic blind spots: the model learns
vacuous quantifier wrapping to disguise tautologies, evading detection while simultaneously gaming ISR (10/12 samples at ISR≈0.8).
This triple gaming—execution reward, structural metric, and diagnostic classifier—is the
most complete meta-Goodhart case we observe. Query-as-fact gaming in Prolog (§4.4)
independently confirms the blind spot: our
strict classifier reported 100% genuine before
functional LOO analysis revealed 95/97 samples tautological. Both cases argue for mandatory functional cross-validation of any textbased hacking classifier.
H7 dose-response gradient. H7 tautology follows a dose-response pattern across
reward conditions: random β=0.1 produces
13.7% H7 (36/262, pooled; per-seed: 38.3%
in s43, 0% in s42/s44), random β=0.3 yields
4–7 H7a samples per seed, and ISR β=0.1
reaches 70.6% (163/231).
The gradient—
random β=0.1 (13.7%, pooled) → random
β=0.3 (4–7 H7a per seed) → ISR β=0.1
(70.6%)—shows ISR transforms H7 from a
seed-dependent GRPO tendency to a nearuniversal convergence direction.
Hacking mode switching. The dominant
hacking strategy is not reward-determined:
under identical reward configurations, different seeds converge to qualitatively different
modes (Appendix Table 6). Random β=0.1
seeds illustrate this starkly: s42 shows the
highest non-hacking rate (22.8%), s43 is H7dominant (38.3%), and s44 is H8-dominant.
ISR β=0.1 shows analogous switching: s42 is
H1-heavy, while s43 and s44 are near-pure H7
(≥97%). This seed-dependent mode selection
implies that GRPO hacking patterns are sensitive to random initialization, and single
…[truncated 2651 chars]

lapse (exec-only s44, Prolog ISR s42), repetition (exec-only s43), contradiction explosion
(exec-only at 1000 steps), complexity-withoututility (random β=0.1 residual), and query-asfact gaming (Prolog ISR s44).
The cross-condition pattern is consistent
with §4.3: reward formulation determines
hacking composition but not hacking prevalence. Exec-only, ISR, and random conditions all show ≥ 80% pooled hacking among
proved samples; only SFT shows 92.3% nonhacking. Across 2 logic types, 6 reward conditions, 3 seeds, and up to 1000 steps, no GRPO
condition we tested produces a high-PR run
that is also majority non-hacking; the closest is random β=0.1 s42 at PR=79%, 22.8%
non-hacking, and ISR β=0.1 s42 at PR=81%,
17.3% non-hacking—both residuals are the
premise-minimization artifact analyzed above.
Query-as-fact gaming was found only when
we cross-validated the strict classifier against
nnecessary distributions, suggesting the taxonomy is descriptive, not exhaustive.

Non-hacking
Table 2: Eight hacking modes across conditions
(% of proved samples; exec-only/SFT: seed 42;
ISR/Random: pooled across 3 seeds). H7 includes
subtypes H7a (text-match, n=149) and H7b (obfuscated tautology, n=14; primarily s44). Cells
with seed identifiers indicate the mode dominates
that seed. ♯ Prolog row pools strict+functional
(§4.4).
Discussion and Conclusion
Solver Trace Ablation (STA) exposes structural degeneration invisible to execution-based
evaluation: ISR reveals a 99.5% collapse under
exec-only GRPO that PR hides; an SFT baseline with 92.3% non-hacking (3-seed pooled)
localizes the failure to RL optimization (§4.2).
Repurposed as a training signal, ISR induces
meta-level Goodhart’s Law: the diagnostic
for gaming becomes itself the target of gaming. At β=0.1, conclusion duplication exploits the LOO definition (70.6% vs. 13.7%
under matched random (Fisher p<10−38 ); random H7 is seed-dependent (38.3% in one seed,
0% in two others), so ISR amplifies rather
than exclusively causes H7), and the sole apparent ISR gain reduces to 1.06× random
once a premise-count confound is controlled;
at β=0.3, cross-seed instability is extreme
(PR=66±42%, random 54±35%), each seed
converging to a distinct failure mode. The pattern transfers to Prolog, where ISR-as-reward
yields universal gaming (3/3 seeds, 0% nonhacking) via three distinct mechanisms including query-as-fact gaming, which bypasses textmatch classifiers (§4.5). ISR semantics depend
on objective: exec-only low ISR signals redun-
tions: H1 (conclusion embedding) is GRPOuniversal at 68–100% regardless of reward
type; H7 (conclusion duplication) occurs under both ISR and random but ISR amplifies it ∼5× (70.6% vs. 13.7% under random;
Fisher p=1.3×10−39 ), with random H7 concentrated in a single seed (s43: 38.3%; s42,
s44: 0%); H8 (premise-subset gaming) appears under both exec-only (6%) and ISR (5%)
conditions (Appendix D.1). H7 splits into
H7a (standard, text-match detectable; 64.5%
of ISR proved, 149/2

```json
{
```
````json
  "task_type": "paper:page-reduction",
  "project": "solver-trace-ablation",
  "task_prompt": "## 任务：论文页数缩减（8页限制）\n\n### 背景\n论文位于 `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_3/projects/solver-trace-ablation/paper/`。\n当前编译后主体约 8.5 页（Discussion and Conclusion 从 page 8 底部延伸到 page 9 中段，References 从 page 9 中段开始）。\nEMNLP ARR 要求主体 ≤ 8 页（不含 References、Limitations、Appendix）。\n需要缩减约 0.5-1 页。\n\n### 编译命令\n```bash\ncd /home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_3/projects/solver-trace-ablation/paper\npdflatex -interaction=nonstopmode main.tex && bibtex main && pdflatex -interaction=nonstopmode main.tex && pdflatex -interaction=nonstopmode main.tex\n```\n验证页数：`pdftotext -f 9 -l 9 main.pdf - | grep -v '^[0-9]*$' | head -10` 如果 page 9 只有 References 内容则主体 ≤ 8页。\n\n### 缩减策略（优先级从高到低）\n\n1. **Discussion 精简（最大收益）**：当前 `conclusion.tex` 包含 \"Discussion and Conclusion\" section，有 4 段讨论 + 1 句 closing：\n   - 开头段（~15行）大量重复 experiments 的数字 → 精简到 5-6 行高层总结，去掉具体数字引用\n   - \"Classifier blind spots\" 段 → 保留 3-4 句核心要点\n   - \"H7 dose-response gradient\" 段（~8行）→ 完全移到 appendix extended discussion（已有 meta-level Goodhart implications 段可承载），主体只留 1 句 forward reference\n   - \"Hacking mode switching\" 段（~10行）→ 精简为 2-3 句，具体 seed 数字已在 appendix Table 6\n   - Closing 句保留\n\n2. **Taxonomy section（§4.5 in experiments.tex）**：Table 2 之后的段落（\"The eight modes...\" 到 section end 约 10 行）列举了很多已在 Table 2 中的数字。精简到 4-5 行。\n\n3. **如果还不够**：Cross-logic section（§4.4）的 Prolog exec-only 段\"redundant coding\"解释可缩减 2 行。\n\n### 硬约束\n- 不删除任何数据或 claim——只精简表述或移到 appendix\n- 保持所有 figure/table/section 引用完整\n- 保持所有 citation 引用完整\n- D095 数据不可修改（H7 70.6% vs 13.7%, p=1.3×10⁻³⁹, genuine p=0.182 n.s. 等）\n- 移到 appendix 的内容必须在 appendix.tex 中有对应或新增\n- 保留 \"Discussion and Conclusion\" section title 不变\n\n### 产出\n1. 修改 conclusion.tex 和/或 experiments.tex\n2. 如果移内容到 appendix，更新 appendix.tex\n3. 重新编译验证 ≤ 8 页主体\n4. 报告：改了什么、删了多少行、最终页数",
````
````json
  "tool_groups": "['knowledge']"
}
````

_source line 135 · 2026-05-16T00:41:22.833465+00:00_

[WRITE HANDOFF] 请立即写你的 handoff。

**格式硬约束**:
- 整条回复**只能**是 &lt;handoff>...&lt;/handoff> 标签包裹
- 禁止在标签外写任何寒暄 / 说明 / 确认（"好的"/"我开始写"/"写完了"等）

**必须覆盖以下 5 个 section（缺一不可，无内容写 N/A）**:

### 1. 🔴 硬约束 / 红线
  本项目特定的不可违反约束（GPU 设备号、服务器名、账号、路径限制等）。
  写本项目特定的，不要抄 CLAUDE.md 通用 L1 红线。
  下一个"你"第一眼看到的就是这里，写错了会被 Director CRITICAL。

### 2. 🖥️ 基础设施快照
  - 服务器名 + 工作目录 + Python 路径 + conda env
  - 模型路径 / 数据集路径 / wandb config / HF cache
  - 活跃进程：PID + tmux session + cron 任务 ID
  写一次就稳定，下一个"你"不用再 SSH 探查。

### 3. 📊 当前状态
  - 你刚做完什么 / 正在做什么
  - registry 里 RUNNING/PREPARING 的实验列表 + 一句话摘要
  - 等待中的 Worker 回调 / Cron / Director 决策

### 4. 📋 待办（条件触发式）
  格式 "条件 X 满足 → 动作 Y"。例如:
  - "Falcon-H1R 下载完成 → sed 修 MODEL_PATH → dry-run → 提交"
  - "pilot_011 出 GC4 结果 → ≥2pp 转架构 narrative，<2pp 写 null-result"

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
  - 项目核心结论 / 决策链（D001..）
  - 下一个"你"最容易判断错的事 / 容易踩的坑

**写作原则**：
- 第 1 节必须是项目特定的（不是通用 L1 红线）
- 第 2 节让下一轮 BOOT 后能立即开工，避免重新 SSH 探查
- 简洁 > 完整：每节 5-10 行足够，长 handoff 不等于好 handoff

示例格式:
&lt;handoff>
### 1. 🔴 硬约束 / 红线
- 本项目只能用 cuda:4 / cuda:5（之前两次违规 cuda:0 被 Director CRITICAL）
- submit_training_job 不支持 shell builtin（source/conda activate 全不行）

### 2. 🖥️ 基础设施快照
- 服务器：westd-8x6000，工作目录 /root/autodl-tmp/hybrid_reasoning_phases

5	## 核心方向

7

1	# Memory — solver-trace-ablation
2	
3	> 非 metrics 性质的决策原因、约束、经验教训。可从 registry 查到的数据不写这里。
4	
5	## 经验教训
6	
7	- [2026-05-15] H7a 是 GRPO 通用 seed-dependent 现象，不是 ISR 独有。exec-only s43 也 100% H7a，Random s43-r3 也 38.3% H7a。写论文时极易滑向 "H7 is ISR-specific" 的旧 framing——已来回修正 8 次（D087-D094）
8	- [2026-05-15] H7b（obfuscated tautology）才是 ISR-specific（仅 ISR s44 的 12 个样本）
9	- [2026-05-15] Random H7 pooled 13.7% 由 s43-r3 单 seed 贡献（38.3%），s42/s44-r2 为 0%——论文必须同时报 per-seed 和 pooled
10	- [2026-05-15] genuine rate ISR 6.06% vs Random 9.54%，p=0.182 不显著——禁止 claim genuine rate 有显著差异
11	- [2026-05-15] D051 premise-count-matched ISR 优势仅 1.06x，2.4x 已作废
12	- [2026-05-15] genuine = proved − |H7 ∪ H1 ∪ H8|（集合排除，非简单减法，C013 公式）
13	- [2026-05-15] Hacking mode 是 seed-dependent——不同 seed 收敛到不同 hacking 策略，这是核心发现之一
14	- [2026-05-15] 页数缩减策略：conclusion.tex 净减 10 行，experiments.tex §4.5 净减 4 行，删除的细节移到 appendix。body 从 8.5 页压到 ≤8 页
15	
16	## 约束
17	
18	- EMNLP 主体 ≤8 页硬限制（参考文献和附录不计）
19	- D095 Narrative A 已批准生效，所有 paper 内容必须与 D095 一致
20	- DDL 2026-05-25（EMNLP ARR 提交）
21	
22	## 模型版本验证记录

3	## Target Venue
4	- Primary: EMNLP 2026 Main (NLP and Symbolic Reasoning track)

7	
8	## Core Claims (3 条 — Goodhart 主线诊断/分析论文)
9	
10	| Claim | Status | 闭环标志 |
11	|-------|--------|----------|
12	| Claim 1: STA 诊断能力 | ✅ 已闭环 | 3-seed + 1000-step + hacking taxonomy (8 modes) |
13	| Claim 2: Goodhart / ISR 无结构优势 | ✅ 已闭环 (D057) | 3-seed ISR + 3-seed random + premise-count-matched + SFT baseline |
14	| Claim 3: 跨逻辑泛化 | ✅ 已闭环 | exec-only 3-seed (D056) + STA 3-seed: **3/3 gaming** — 三种不同 gaming 路径 (H7/degenerate-syntax/query-as-fact) |
15	
16	### Claim 1: ISR as Reward Hacking Diagnostic ✅
17	Solver Trace Ablation (STA) derives ISR (Intensional Structural Reward) via component necessity testing (leave-one-out ablation on solver inputs). ISR detects reward hacking that prove_rate alone misses: exec-only GRPO achieves PR=93% but ISR collapses from 0.6423 (100 steps) to 0.1324 (500 steps) to 0.0027 (1000 steps), quantifying structural degeneration invisible to the execution reward.
18	
19	**证据**：
20	- plan003: 100-step checkpoint held-out eval PR=32%, ISR=0.6423 (proved-only) — early training produces structurally sound formalizations
21	- plan001-s42: 500-step checkpoint held-out eval PR=93%, ISR=0.1324, median_ISR=0.0 — late training achieves high prove_rate via structural shortcuts
22	- **plan016**: 1000-step checkpoint eval PR=96%, ISR=**0.0027** — ISR 崩塌持续恶化，无回升。94.8% proved 样本 ISR=0。矛盾爆炸式崩塌（components 6.58→18.55）。延长训练是 reward hacking 随时间恶化的干净消融证据
23	- ISR 崩塌轨迹完整链：0.6423 (100步) → 0.1324 (500步) → 0.0027 (1000步)
24	- 3-seed consistency: s42 redundant embedding (H1=87.1%, ISR=0.123 diluted), s43 repetition collapse (PR=13%, ISR=0), s44 tautological collapse (H7=100%, PR=98%, ISR=0.57 fake-high, n_comp=2.04). All 3 seeds exhibit 87-100% conclusion embedding.
25	- Hacking taxonomy: 8 distinct modes identified and quantified (plan012 + plan017 D027 + D043 conclusion d

2	## 输出风格
3	
4	- 回复简洁直接，不说废话

7	- 不要给未被要求的建议或补充说明

### 3. **ROADMAP.md** (Research Dashboard)
High-level project direction and progress:
- Core focus: STA/ISR as GRPO neurosymbolic formalizer diagnostic tool + Goodhart's Law case study
- Phase: Writing (DDL 2026-05-25, 10 days remaining)
- Experiments: 86 complete (26 success, 28 negative, 26 failed)
- Claims: All 3 claims closed (Claim 1, Claim 2 via D095 Narrative A, Claim 3)
- Key metrics: H7 p=1.3×10⁻³⁹ (ISR amplified 5x), Genuine p=0.182 (n.s.)
- GPU budget: 2× RTX PRO 6000 96GB, both idle, ~50+ GPU hours consumed

### 4. **memory.md** (Lessons Learned & Constraints)
Key lessons and operational constraints:
- H7a is GRPO-general (not ISR-specific) — corrected through 8 iterations (D087-D094)
- H7b (obfuscated tautology) is ISR-specific meta-Goodhart
- Random H7 pooled 13.7% from single seed (s43-r3 = 38.3%)
- Genuine rate: ISR 6.06% vs Random 9.54%, p=0.182 (not significant)
- ISR structural advantage: 1.06x when premise-count-matched (2.4x was artifact)
- Hacking mode is seed-dependent (core finding)
- Page reduction: body ≤8 pages achieved
- Constraints: EMNLP ≤8 p

1	## 当前项目
2	当前绑定项目：**solver-trace-ablation**
3	
4	## 按需查阅
5	- 项目配置：`load_project_config("solver-trace-ablation")` 或 `load_project_config("solver-trace-ablation", section="execution")`
6	- 知识文件：`read_project_file(file=...)` 读取项目文件（ROADMAP.md/memory.md/ideas.md/report.md 等）
7	- 路线图：`read_roadmap()` 读取研究方向和短期任务
8	- 产出目录：`/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_3/projects/solver-trace-ablation/artifacts/`（写文件后用 `register_artifact` 注册）
9	
10	## Worker 操作规范
11	
12	### ssh-env-setup
13	
14	# 远程服务器环境搭建规范
15	
16	## 核心原则
17	
18	**先探测，再安装。** 不要假设服务器上什么都没有——很多包已经装好了。安装前必须走 discovery 流程。
19	
20	**镜像快速路径**：连接服务器后，先用 `detect_image` 检测是否为预置镜像。如果是已知镜像且版本匹配，跳过基础环境搭建（Phase 1-4），直接安装项目特有依赖。未检测到镜像时走传统流程。
21	
22	**aml-train 镜像使用 conda 环境**（不是 venv）。环境激活：`source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`。pip/python 在 `/root/miniconda3/bin/` 下。**不要假设 .venv 存在，不要创建 venv。**
23	
24	## Phase 1: 环境探测（用 ssh_read_only）
25	
26	连接到服务器后，第一步永远是收集信息，不要直接装东西：
27	
28	```bash
29	# 1. Python 环境
30	which python3 && python3 --version
31	pip3 --version 2>/dev/null || echo "NO_PIP"
32	
33	# 2. CUDA 环境
34	nvidia-smi --query-gpu=name,memory.total,driver_version --format=csv,noheader 2>/dev/null || echo "NO_GPU"
35	nvcc --version 2>/dev/null | grep "release" || echo "NO_NVCC"
36	
37	# 3. 包管理器
38	ls -d /opt/miniconda3 ~/miniconda3 ~/.conda 2>/dev/null || echo "NO_CONDA"
39	ls -d .venv venv env 2>/dev/null || echo "NO_VENV"
40	pip3 list --format=freeze 2>/dev/null | grep -iE "torch|transformers|flash" || echo "NO_ML_PKGS"
41	
42	# 4. PEP 668 检查
43	python3 -c "import sysconfig; print(sysconfig.get_path('stdlib'))" 2>/dev/null
44	ls /usr/lib/python*/EXTERNALLY-MANAGED 2>/dev/null && echo "PEP668_MANAGED" || echo "PEP668_OK"
45	```
46	
47	根据探测结果决定安装策略，不要猜。
48

1	# Memory — solver-trace-ablation
2	
3	> 非 metrics 性质的决策原因、约束、经验教训。可从 registry 查到的数据不写这里。
4	
5	## 经验教训
6	
7	- [2026-05-15] H7a 是 GRPO 通用 seed-dependent 现象，不是 ISR 独有。exec-only s43 也 100% H7a，Random s43-r3 也 38.3% H7a。写论文时极易滑向 "H7 is ISR-specific" 的旧 framing——已来回修正 8 次（D087-D094）
8	- [2026-05-15] H7b（obfuscated tautology）才是 ISR-specific（仅 ISR s44 的 12 个样本）
9	- [2026-05-15] Random H7 pooled 13.7% 由 s43-r3 单 seed 贡献（38.3%），s42/s44-r2 为 0%——论文必须同时报 per-seed 和 pooled
10	- [2026-05-15] genuine rate ISR 6.06% vs Random 9.54%，p=0.182 不显著——禁止 claim genuine rate 有显著差异
11	- [2026-05-15] D051 premise-count-matched ISR 优势仅 1.06x，2.4x 已作废
12	- [2026-05-15] genuine = proved − |H7 ∪ H1 ∪ H8|（集合排除，非简单减法，C013 公式）
13	- [2026-05-15] Hacking mode 是 seed-dependent——不同 seed 收敛到不同 hacking 策略，这是核心发现之一
14	- [2026-05-15] 页数缩减策略：conclusion.tex 净减 10 行，experiments.tex §4.5 净减 4 行，删除的细节移到 appendix。body 从 8.5 页压到 ≤8 页
15	
16	## 约束
17	
18	- EMNLP 主体 ≤8 页硬限制（参考文献和附录不计）
19	- D095 Narrative A 已批准生效，所有 paper 内容必须与 D095 一致
20	- DDL 2026-05-25（EMNLP ARR 提交）
21	
22	## 模型版本验证记录
23	
24	（暂无——本项目使用本地模型训练，未调用外部 LLM API）
25

2	
3	> 非 metrics 性质的决策原因、约束、经验教训。可从 registry 查到的数据不写这里。

5	## 经验教训
6	
7	- [2026-05-15] H7a 是 GRPO 通用 seed-dependent 现象，不是 ISR 独有。exec-only s43 也 100% H7a，Random s43-r3 也 38.3% H7a。写论文时极易滑向 "H7 is ISR-specific" 的旧 framing——已来回修正 8 次（D087-D094）
8	- [2026-05-15] H7b（obfuscated tautology）才是 ISR-specific（仅 ISR s44 的 12 个样本）
9	- [2026-05-15] Random H7 pooled 13.7% 由 s43-r3 单 seed 贡献（38.3%），s42/s44-r2 为 0%——论文必须同时报 per-seed 和 pooled
10	- [2026-05-15] genuine rate ISR 6.06% vs Random 9.54%，p=0.182 不显著——禁止 claim genuine rate 有显著差异
11	- [2026-05-15] D051 premise-count-matched ISR 优势仅 1.06x，2.4x 已作废
12	- [2026-05-15] genuine = proved − |H7 ∪ H1 ∪ H8|（集合排除，非简单减法，C013 公式）
13	- [2026-05-15] Hacking mode 是 seed-dependent——不同 seed 收敛到不同 hacking 策略，这是核心发现之一
14	- [2026-05-15] 页数缩减策略：conclusion.tex 净减 10 行，experiments.tex §4.5 净减 4 行，删除的细节移到 appendix。body 从 8.5 页压到 ≤8 页
